HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_09b531a75203e9a354fc","text":"No later ayah occurs in this fixed ayah's clipped focus window, so no later surprise or correction emerged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_19634f0e1432eb7cb850","text":"The whole packet made the last ayah function as proof, not appendix. The opening `د ي ن` branches and the middle food/prayer branches converge here: debt, mercy, household, flow, and attention are all tested by whether a person lets small benefit pass.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001448/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_230d545d83acb4ff176e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:7:1\",\"root_001448\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Koruma gücü tersine çevrilir: korunmasız kişiyi gözetebilecek imkân, kaynakları o kişiden koruyan bir kaleye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001448/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_75f22414c5ac1f7c0780","text":"2. **Blocking the flow.** Evidence: `م ن ع B002` barrier between seeker and sought, `م ع ن B001` flowing visible water, and `م ع ن B005` including water where named; activated by `ط ع م B001/B002` food and feeding, and `س ك ن B010` provisions enabling staying. Structure: food, dwelling-provision, water/benefit form a flow of life-support. Mechanism: the actor interrupts circulation, turning flow into blockage. Abductive move: I supply the hydraulic/social-flow model; the packet supplies water-flow, feeding, provision, and barrier branches. Reading change: يمنعون الماعون becomes damming the small streams by which a community lives.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001448/B002","root_004706/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_93f38b199b71ec7c5028","text":"2. **Withholding is a barrier that dams a necessary flow.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `م ن ع/B002` is a barrier between a person and what is sought; fixed `م ع ن/B001` is visible flowing water, including a small running stream. **Structural cue:** the barrier-verb directly governs the flow-capable object after 107:3's unmet need for food. **Assigned role:** `منع` is the obstruction and `ماعون` is the social current that should pass through ordinary channels. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state hydraulic causation; I supply the directional arrow that a barrier stops a flow. **Reading change:** `يمنعون الماعون` becomes a circulation failure: assistance is not absent at source but deliberately dammed before reaching need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B002","root_004706/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9c486752eb108abad15c","text":"3. **Refusing household access.** Evidence: `م ع ن B005` household benefit/lent tools, `م ع ن B006` dwelling/lodging-place; activated by `س ك ن B002` dwelling and `س ك ن B003` household inhabitants. Structure: the last noun is domestic; earlier miskīn/orphan scenes show exclusion from settled household care. Mechanism: withholding maʿūn seals the house against those who need its ordinary implements. Abductive move: I infer domestic closure; the packet supplies household, dwelling, and withholding branches. Reading change: the ayah reads as refusal of neighborly infrastructure, not merely refusal of charity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001448/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ad9adf29ee1d32bdcd81","text":"4. **Possessive protection inverted into selfish fortress.** Evidence: `م ن ع B003` protective strength/fortress, activated by `ي ت م B001` lacking protector and `ر ح م B002` kinship bond. Structure: the vulnerable need protective manʿah; the actor uses manʿ as private defense of goods. Mechanism: protection that should guard weak kin becomes fortification against them. Abductive move: I supply the inversion of protection into hoarding; the packet supplies protection, kinship, orphaning, and withholding. Reading change: يمنعون is ambivalent: the same root that could protect instead excludes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B010","root_004706/B003","root_004706/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae7e83a4affba19131ae","text":"4. **A slight household act can have dwelling-scale consequences.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `م ع ن/B003` is ease, smallness, and light weight; fixed `م ع ن/B005` includes minor household utility, and fixed `م ع ن/B006` is a dwelling or stopping-place. Nearby `س ك ن/B010` is provision that enables staying. **Structural cue:** the surah moves from food and poverty to refusal of the smallest domestic-scale utility. **Assigned role:** smallness measures low cost to the holder, while the dwelling branches measure high stabilizing value to the recipient. **Abductive move:** the packet does not quantify either side; I infer the asymmetry by joining “slight/easy” benefit to provision-enabled staying. **Reading change:** `الماعون` is not trivial residue: it is low-cost social infrastructure whose denial can destabilize a household.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001448/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b9e349c1459f3f5668d4","text":"1. **The verb formally encloses and cancels the benefit.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `م ن ع/B001` is holding back the hand from giving, and fixed `م ع ن/B005` is `الماعون` as small available household benefit, loan, gift, kindness, or alms. **Structural cue:** the adjacent roots contain the same three consonants with `ن` and `ع` reordered, reinforced by the surface echo of `يمنعون الماعون`. **Assigned role:** the first word names obstruction and the second names what should be available, making act and object a compact opposed pair. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies roots, order, and scopes; activated by `د ي ن B003` debt/credit and `د ي ن B002` reckoning.
